## Best date plans

### 2026-11-28 — Beginners + Lightscape Lodge at Yellow Magnolia Café

**Beginners, then a meal.** Seasonal American at Lightscape Lodge at Yellow Magnolia Café. It is a 20-minute walk from the venue. It starts at 12:15 pm, which leaves the evening for the table.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=188+Prospect+Park+West%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY&destination=Brooklyn+Botanic+Garden%3B+enter+at+990+Washington+Avenue&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://nitehawkcinema.com/prospectpark/movies/beginners/) · [Event booking](https://nitehawkcinema.com/prospectpark/purchase/24875721/) · [Restaurant](https://www.bbg.org/feature/lightscape/lightscape_lodge_dinners) · [Reservations](https://resy.com/cities/new-york-ny/venues/yellow-magnolia-cafe)

### 2026-11-28 — Brooklyn Flea Holiday Market — Saturday + La Mestiza

**Brooklyn Flea Holiday Market — Saturday, then a meal.** Ecuadorian and Latin American bakery at La Mestiza. It is a 21-minute walk from the venue. It starts at 10 am, which leaves the evening for the table.

### 2026-11-29 — Brooklyn Flea Holiday Market — Sunday + Azar

**Brooklyn Flea Holiday Market — Sunday, then a meal.** Persian street grill at Azar. It is 1.5 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. It starts at 10 am, which leaves the evening for the table.

### 2026-11-28 — 23rd Annual Animation Show of Shows with Bill Plympton + Eyval

**23rd Annual Animation Show of Shows with Bill Plympton, then dinner.** Persian at Eyval. It is a 1-minute walk from the venue. It starts at 5:50 pm, so eat afterwards.

**Dinner, then Museum Hours with Jem Cohen.** Persian street grill at Azar. It is 1.6 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. Museum Hours with Jem Cohen starts at 7:15 pm, so book the table early.

## Where to eat

NYC dining for November 28, 2026: happy-hour offers, set lunches, November openings and upcoming bakery pop-ups. Weekday-only offers and future events are labeled separately; holiday availability is not guaranteed.

Dining research: 2026-11-28T10:38:53+00:00

No current Michelin awards are asserted. Junoon's official location page describes a historical eight-year star run, not a verified current distinction: https://home.junoonnyc.com/en/restaurant-indian-cuisine/west-24th-street-new-york/. Michelin Guide selection does not establish a star. No Michelin primary-source listing was checked within the 12-follow-up limit.

### Le Gratin: weekday prix-fixe lunch and separate bar specials

Deals & happy hours — Le Gratin

Lunch offers two or three courses. A separate evening bar offer features steak, mussels, chicken or steak tartare with fries.

Price: Lunch: $45 for two courses or $55 for three. Bar entrées: $30 each; mini martinis: $8 separately.  
Schedule: Lunch Monday–Friday 11:30am–2:30pm. Bar specials Tuesday–Friday 5–7pm.  
Conditions: Neither offer applies on Saturday November 28. Evening specials are bar-only; walk-ins welcome. Lunch supplements include tartare +$5, skate +$3, burger +$5 and steak frites +$8. Reservations offered; lunch booking requirement and any additional minimum purchase are unspecified. Tax, tip and service charges unspecified.  
[Source](https://legratinnyc.com/our-menu/lunch)

### Mermaid Oyster Bar Times Square: $2 shellfish and $1.50 late oysters

Deals & happy hours — Mermaid Oyster Bar Times Square

Useful pre-theater seafood specials include oysters, littlenecks and small snacks. A later oyster offer is restricted to the bar.

Price: Happy hour: chef-choice oysters or littlenecks $2 each, minimum six; mini fish taco or chowder $4; selected cocktails and wines $12; beers $9. Late chef-choice oysters $1.50 each.  
Schedule: Happy-hour menu lists Monday 3:30pm–close and Tuesday–Sunday 3:30–6:30pm. Late oysters nightly 7pm–close; closing time not independently reverified.  
Conditions: Happy-hour oysters and littlenecks have a six-piece minimum. Late chef-choice oysters are bar-only; no late-offer minimum is stated. Do not extend the happy-hour minimum to the late offer by assumption. Holiday availability, other minimums, tax, tip and service charges unconfirmed.  
[Source](https://themermaidnyc.getbento.com/the-mermaid-inn-times-square-menus/)

### Vin Sur Vingt Midtown East: $40 early prix fixe and wine specials

Deals & happy hours — Vin Sur Vingt Bistro Midtown East

The early menu lists soup or country pâté followed by mussels with fries or salmon. Separate wine promotions serve early and late diners.

Price: Prix fixe $40; dessert +$10. Selected wine glasses $10. Any wine bottle 25% off during the late window.  
Schedule: Prix fixe 4–7pm; selected glasses opening–6pm; bottle discount 10pm–close. Opens daily at 4pm; closes midnight Sunday–Wednesday and 1am Thursday–Saturday.  
Conditions: No weekday restriction is listed. The HTML prix fixe lists soup or pâté, then mussels or salmon. Its linked June 2026 PDF additionally lists endive salad and a burger, with differing dessert choices; current selection is unresolved. Both sources agree on $40, dessert +$10 and 4–7pm. Wine discounts are separate. Booking requirements, minimum purchases, tax, tip and service charges unspecified.  
[Source](https://www.vsvwinebars.com/location/vin-sur-vingt-wine-bar-bistro-e-51st-st/)

Schedule: Every night 5–6pm at the bar. Menu also lists Wednesday–Sunday 3–5pm.  
Conditions: The 5–6pm offer is bar-only. Seating restrictions for the earlier window are unspecified, and that window is not repeated on the location page. Oyster minimum, reservation requirements, tax, tip and service charges unspecified.

#### [Elektra: newly extended final week](https://tfana.org/events/elektra-2026-11-28-200-pm)

2026-12-08 · 7:30 PM · theater · Regular tickets $70–$98 plus $7 handling; premium and eligibility-based discounts priced separately. · Polonsky Shakespeare Center · Fort Greene · Brooklyn · Score 59.0  
Daniel Fish directs Sophokles' tragedy in Anne Carson's translation, with music by Ted Hearne. The production has added eight performances for December 8–13.
**Timing:** December 8 performance starts at 7:30pm. Newly extended performances continue through December 13; running time approximately 80 minutes.
**Practical note:** No late seating. Bright lights are used. Regular tickets are within the $120 budget; premium tickets exceed it.
**Why now:** New extension dates offer alternatives to the depleted original run.
**Hot booking alert:** The official calendar marks December 8–13 as newly extended, while December 5–6 performances have limited-to-no availability or are sold out. December 13 is the announced closing date, not a separate sales cutoff.
